- **britch**: Britch is the adversarial idea gate — he critiques a project idea BEFORE any time is invested, interrogates it with hard questions, names the weakest assumption, and returns exactly one verdict: KILL, SHARPEN FIRST, or PROCEED (with kill criteria). - **bro**: Session continuity for Claude Code. One flow for every kind of record - journal, harvest, log, expire, archive - and hooks that print the live records (rules, decisions, tasks, operator state, glossary, insights) plus the clock into every new, resumed, compacted or cleared chat. The chat writes the day's journal through one script (bro-append.sh) using plain markers like TASK: and DECISION:; scripts do the rest, no model involved.
